\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{main_8c_source_l02352}02352\ \textcolor{comment}{/**}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{main_8c_source_l02353}02353\ \textcolor{comment}{\ *\ @brief\ Algoritmo\ de\ seguimiento\ de\ línea\ con\ guiñada\ no\ lineal,\ cruce\ y\ navegación\ a\ ciegas\ (Dead\ Reckoning).}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{main_8c_source_l02354}02354\ \textcolor{comment}{\ *\ @details\ Implementa\ la\ cinemática\ de\ guiñada\ del\ robot\ sobre\ pista:}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{main_8c_source_l02355}02355\ \textcolor{comment}{\ *\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ 1.\ **Filtro\ y\ Discriminación\ Óptica**:\ Binariza\ las\ lecturas\ analógicas\ normalizadas\ de\ los\ 3\ fotosensores\ frontales\ (`IR\_WHITE`).}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{main_8c_source_l02356}02356\ \textcolor{comment}{\ *\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ 2.\ **Estimación\ de\ Centroide**:\ Calcula\ el\ error\ de\ desviación\ lateral\ \$e\_\{line\}\ \(\backslash\)in\ [-\/100,\ +100]\$\ mediante\ el\ centro\ de\ masa\ óptico\ normalizado\ por\ la\ suma\ de\ reflectancia.}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{main_8c_source_l02357}02357\ \textcolor{comment}{\ *\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ 3.\ **Control\ No\ Lineal\ P\ +\ Cuadrático**:\ Sintetiza\ el\ par\ de\ guiñada\ combinando\ una\ acción\ proporcional\ suave\ para\ rectas\ y\ un\ término\ cuadrático\ (\$K\_\{q\(\backslash\)\_line\}\ \(\backslash\)cdot\ e\_\{line\}\ \(\backslash\)cdot\ |e\_\{line\}|\$)\ con\ alta\ autoridad\ correctiva\ en\ curvas\ cerradas.}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{main_8c_source_l02358}02358\ \textcolor{comment}{\ *\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ 4.\ **Filtro\ de\ Desenganche**:\ Temporizador\ de\ rebote\ de\ 6\ ciclos\ (30\ ms)\ para\ ignorar\ huecos\ o\ discontinuidades\ menores\ en\ la\ línea.}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{main_8c_source_l02359}02359\ \textcolor{comment}{\ *\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ 5.\ **Maniobra\ de\ Dead\ Reckoning\ en\ Pérdida**:\ En\ desenganche\ total,\ utiliza\ el\ giróscopo\ Z\ integrado\ para\ ejecutar\ un\ patrón\ de\ búsqueda\ angular\ (+90°\ \$\(\backslash\)rightarrow\$\ espera\ 2s\ \$\(\backslash\)rightarrow\$\ -\/180°\ \$\(\backslash\)rightarrow\$\ frenado\ estático\ a\ -\/2.5°\ en\ rampa).}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{main_8c_source_l02360}02360\ \textcolor{comment}{\ *\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ 6.\ **Resolución\ de\ Cruces\ en\ Cruz**:\ En\ bifurcaciones/cruces\ de\ 3\ sensores,\ aplica\ jitter\ estocástico\ con\ el\ registro\ SysTick\ para\ desempatar\ y\ pivotar\ sobre\ el\ propio\ eje.}}

\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{main_8c_source_l02613}02613\ \textcolor{comment}{\ *\ @brief\ Control\ supervisor\ de\ equilibrio\ estático\ longitudinal\ con\ detección\ por\ histéresis\ de\ caída\ inminente.}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{main_8c_source_l02614}02614\ \textcolor{comment}{\ *\ @details\ Gestiona\ el\ modo\ estacionario\ de\ balanceo\ puro\ (`STATE\_SWING`\ y\ \`{}STATE\_3D\_SCREEN`):}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{main_8c_source_l02615}02615\ \textcolor{comment}{\ *\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ 1.\ **Neutralización\ de\ Guiñada**:\ Cancela\ cualquier\ par\ de\ rotación\ (`turn\_offset\ =\ 0`)\ para\ aislar\ el\ lazo\ de\ balance\ longitudinal.}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{main_8c_source_l02616}02616\ \textcolor{comment}{\ *\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ 2.\ **Consigna\ Nominal**:\ Carga\ la\ consigna\ estática\ calibrada\ \`{}setpoint`\ como\ referencia\ de\ equilibrio\ erguido.}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{main_8c_source_l02617}02617\ \textcolor{comment}{\ *\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ 3.\ **Detector\ Schmitt\ Trigger\ de\ Caída\ Frontal**:\ Monitorea\ el\ ángulo\ pitch\ estimado\ \`{}current\_angle`:}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{main_8c_source_l02618}02618\ \textcolor{comment}{\ *\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ -\/\ Si\ la\ inclinación\ frontal\ supera\ los\ \$-\/15.00\string^\(\backslash\)circ\$\ (`current\_angle\ <\ -\/ANG15`),\ activa\ \`{}forwards\_recovery\_active\ =\ 1`.}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{main_8c_source_l02619}02619\ \textcolor{comment}{\ *\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ -\/\ Aplica\ una\ histéresis\ de\ \$5.00\string^\(\backslash\)circ\$,\ desactivando\ la\ recuperación\ únicamente\ cuando\ el\ robot\ retorna\ a\ \$\(\backslash\)ge\ -\/10.00\string^\(\backslash\)circ\$\ (`current\_angle\ >=\ -\/ANG10`).}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{main_8c_source_l02620}02620\ \textcolor{comment}{\ *\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ 4.\ **Clamping\ de\ Consigna\ en\ Caída**:\ Cuando\ la\ bandera\ de\ recuperación\ está\ activa,\ fuerza\ \`{}*target\_setpoint\ =\ 0`\ para\ suprimir\ la\ consigna\ de\ avance\ y\ permitir\ que\ el\ par\ reactivo\ levante\ el\ chasis.}}

\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{main_8c_source_l03045}03045\ \textcolor{comment}{/**}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{main_8c_source_l03046}03046\ \textcolor{comment}{\ *\ @brief\ Lazo\ PID\ central\ de\ balance\ longitudinal\ a\ 200\ Hz\ con\ Derivative-\/on-\/Measurement,\ Anti-\/Windup\ con\ Fuga,\ Mezclador\ Diferencial\ y\ PWM.}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{main_8c_source_l03047}03047\ \textcolor{comment}{\ *\ @details\ Ejecuta\ el\ cómputo\ crítico\ en\ tiempo\ real\ del\ péndulo\ invertido\ cada\ 5\ ms\ (\$DT\(\backslash\)\_US\ =\ 5000\(\backslash\)\ \(\backslash\)mu\(\backslash\)text\{s\}\$):}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{main_8c_source_l03048}03048\ \textcolor{comment}{\ *\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ 1.\ **Filtro\ y\ Estado\ Angular**:\ Adquiere\ el\ ángulo\ pitch\ \`{}current\_angle`\ estimado\ por\ el\ filtro\ complementario\ inercial\ (giroscopio\ MPU-\/6050\ +\ acelerómetro).}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{main_8c_source_l03049}03049\ \textcolor{comment}{\ *\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ 2.\ **Cálculo\ del\ Error**:\ \$e\ =\ target\(\backslash\)\_setpoint\ -\/\ current\(\backslash\)\_angle\$.}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{main_8c_source_l03050}03050\ \textcolor{comment}{\ *\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ 3.\ **Acción\ Proporcional\ (P)**:\ \$P\ =\ K\_\{p\(\backslash\)\_stable\}\ \(\backslash\)cdot\ e\$.}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{main_8c_source_l03051}03051\ \textcolor{comment}{\ *\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ 4.\ **Acción\ Derivativa\ sobre\ Medición\ (D)**:}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{main_8c_source_l03052}03052\ \textcolor{comment}{\ *\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ -\/\ Implica\ derivar\ la\ medición\ angular\ en\ lugar\ del\ error:\ \$\(\backslash\)frac\{d\}\{dt\}(last\(\backslash\)\_angle\ -\/\ current\(\backslash\)\_angle)\$\ para\ erradicar\ por\ completo\ el\ choque\ o\ "{}Derivative\ Kick"{}\ ante\ cambios\ escalón\ de\ consigna:}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{main_8c_source_l03053}03053\ \textcolor{comment}{\ *\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \$\$D\ =\ K\_\{d\(\backslash\)\_stable\}\ \(\backslash\)cdot\ \(\backslash\)left(\(\backslash\)frac\{(last\(\backslash\)\_angle\ -\/\ current\(\backslash\)\_angle)\ \(\backslash\)cdot\ 10\string^6\}\{DT\(\backslash\)\_US\}\(\backslash\)right)\$\$}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{main_8c_source_l03054}03054\ \textcolor{comment}{\ *\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ 5.\ **Acción\ Integral\ con\ Clamping\ y\ Decaimiento\ Exponencial\ (Anti-\/Windup\ Leaky)**:}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{main_8c_source_l03055}03055\ \textcolor{comment}{\ *\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ -\/\ En\ zona\ lineal\ (\$|e|\ <\ 1.50\string^\(\backslash\)circ\$\ [150\ milígrados]),\ acumula:\ \$integral\ \(\backslash\)mathrel\{+\}=\ \(\backslash\)frac\{e\ \(\backslash\)cdot\ DT\(\backslash\)\_US\}\{1000\}\$\ con\ saturación\ estricta\ a\ \$\(\backslash\)pm(20\ \(\backslash\)cdot\ ANG20)\$\ (\$\(\backslash\)pm\ 40.0\string^\(\backslash\)circ\$\ equiv).}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{main_8c_source_l03056}03056\ \textcolor{comment}{\ *\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ -\/\ Fuera\ de\ la\ zona\ lineal\ (\$|e|\ \(\backslash\)ge\ 1.50\string^\(\backslash\)circ\$),\ aplica\ una\ fuga\ exponencial\ del\ 20\%\ por\ ciclo\ (\$integral\ =\ \(\backslash\)frac\{integral\ \(\backslash\)cdot\ 8\}\{10\}\$)\ para\ atenuar\ instantáneamente\ la\ memoria\ inercial\ y\ prevenir\ sobrepasos\ descontrolados\ al\ estabilizarse.}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{main_8c_source_l03057}03057\ \textcolor{comment}{\ *\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ -\/\ Término\ integral:\ \$I\ =\ \(\backslash\)frac\{K\_\{i\(\backslash\)\_stable\}\ \(\backslash\)cdot\ integral\}\{1000\}\$.}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{main_8c_source_l03058}03058\ \textcolor{comment}{\ *\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ 6.\ **Suma\ Total\ del\ Esfuerzo\ Longitudinal**:}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{main_8c_source_l03059}03059\ \textcolor{comment}{\ *\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \$\$output\ =\ \(\backslash\)frac\{P\ +\ I\ +\ D\}\{10000\}\$\$}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{main_8c_source_l03060}03060\ \textcolor{comment}{\ *\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ 7.\ **Mezclador\ de\ Tracción\ y\ Guiñada**:}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{main_8c_source_l03061}03061\ \textcolor{comment}{\ *\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ -\/\ En\ giros\ pivot\ (búsqueda\ de\ línea\ 90°/180°\ o\ joystick),\ ejecuta\ cancelación\ algebraica\ de\ la\ rueda\ interior\ (\$turn\(\backslash\)\_offset\ =\ \(\backslash\)pm\ \(\backslash\)max(output,\ 150)\$)\ frenándola\ a\ 0\ PWM\ mientras\ la\ rueda\ exterior\ entrega\ el\ doble\ de\ tracción.}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{main_8c_source_l03062}03062\ \textcolor{comment}{\ *\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ -\/\ En\ translación\ normal,\ suma\ diferencialmente\ el\ par\ de\ guiñada:\ \$pwm\_L\ =\ base\_L\ -\/\ turn\(\backslash\)\_offset\$\ y\ \$pwm\_R\ =\ base\_R\ +\ turn\(\backslash\)\_offset\$.}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{main_8c_source_l03063}03063\ \textcolor{comment}{\ *\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ 8.\ **Compensación\ de\ Zona\ Muerta\ (Deadband)**:\ Inyecta\ el\ par\ mínimo\ de\ arranque\ estático\ (\$\(\backslash\)pm\ minPWM\ \(\backslash\)pm\ offset\$)\ acorde\ al\ sentido\ de\ tracción.}}
\DoxyCodeLine{\Hypertarget{main_8c_source_l03064}03064\ \textcolor{comment}{\ *\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ 9.\ **Protección\ Absoluta\ y\ Clamping**:}}
